	tile_slot IO {
		bel_slot IO_INT: routing;
		bel_slot IO[0]: IO;
		bel_slot IO[1]: IO;
		bel_slot IO[2]: IO;
		bel_slot IO[3]: IO;

		tile_class IO_W4 {
			cell CELL[0];
			cell CELL[1];
			cell CELL[2];

			switchbox IO_INT {
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_IO_ECLK[0] = CELL[0].ECLK[0] | CELL[0].ECLK[1];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_IO_ECLK[1] = CELL[0].ECLK[0] | CELL[0].ECLK[1];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_IO_ECLK[2] = CELL[0].ECLK[0] | CELL[0].ECLK[1];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_IO_ECLK[3] = CELL[0].ECLK[0] | CELL[0].ECLK[1];
			}

			bel IO[0] {
				input CLK = CELL[2].IMUX_CLK[0];
				input LSR = CELL[2].IMUX_LSR[0];
				input CE = CELL[2].IMUX_CE[0];
				input ECLK = CELL[0].IMUX_IO_ECLK[0];
				input SLIP = CELL[2].IMUX_B[1];
				input TXDATA[0] = CELL[2].IMUX_A[0];
				input TXDATA[1] = CELL[2].IMUX_C[0];
				input TXDATA[2] = CELL[2].IMUX_D[0];
				input TXDATA[3] = CELL[2].IMUX_A[1];
				input TXDATA[4] = CELL[2].IMUX_A[3];
				input TXDATA[5] = CELL[2].IMUX_C[3];
				input TXDATA[6] = CELL[2].IMUX_D[3];
				input TSDATA[0] = CELL[2].IMUX_B[0];
				input TSDATA[1] = CELL[2].IMUX_C[1];
				input DIRECTION = CELL[2].IMUX_A[2];
				input LOADN = CELL[2].IMUX_C[2];
				input MOVE = CELL[2].IMUX_B[2];
				output INFF = CELL[2].OUT_F[4];
				output INCK = CELL[0].OUT_IO_INCK[0];
				output DI = CELL[2].OUT_F[5];
				output RXDATA[0] = CELL[2].OUT_F[0];
				output RXDATA[1] = CELL[2].OUT_F[1];
				output RXDATA[2] = CELL[2].OUT_F[2];

		tile_class PLL_SW {
			cell CELL[0];
			cell CELL[1];
			cell IO_WE;
			cell IO_IN;

			switchbox BEL_INT {
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_WE.OUT_IO_INCK[0] | IO_WE.OUT_IO_INCK[2] | IO_IN.OUT_IO_INCK[2];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[1] | IO_WE.OUT_IO_INCK[0] | IO_WE.OUT_IO_INCK[2] | IO_IN.OUT_IO_INCK[2];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0] = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB[0] | CELL[1].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_WE.ECLK[0] | IO_WE.ECLK[1];
			}

			bel PLL[0] {
				input CLKFB = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0];
				input RST = CELL[0].IMUX_B[1];
				input CLK[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[0];
				input CLK[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[1];
				input SEL = CELL[0].IMUX_B[2];
				input PHASEDIR = CELL[0].IMUX_D[4];
				input PHASESEL[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_B[4];
				input PHASESEL[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_A[4];
				input PHASESTEP = CELL[0].IMUX_C[4];
				input PHASELOADREG = CELL[0].IMUX_D[3];
				input PLLWAKESYNC = CELL[0].IMUX_C[2];
				input STDBY = CELL[0].IMUX_LSR[0];
				input ENCLKOP = CELL[0].IMUX_D[2];
				input ENCLKOS = CELL[0].IMUX_A[3];
				input ENCLKOS2 = CELL[0].IMUX_B[3];
				input ENCLKOS3 = CELL[0].IMUX_C[3];
				output CLKOP = CELL[0].OUT_F[0], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0];
				output CLKOS = CELL[0].OUT_F[2], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS[0];
				output CLKINTFB = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB[0];
				output LOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[2];
				output CLKOS2 = CELL[0].OUT_F[4], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS2[0];
				output CLKOS3 = CELL[0].OUT_F[6], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS3[0];
				output REFCLK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[0];
				output INTLOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[4];
			}

			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_A[3]              PLL[0].ENCLKOS
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_A[4]              PLL[0].PHASESEL[1]
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_B[1]              PLL[0].RST
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_B[2]              PLL[0].SEL
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_B[3]              PLL[0].ENCLKOS2
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_B[4]              PLL[0].PHASESEL[0]
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_C[2]              PLL[0].PLLWAKESYNC
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_C[3]              PLL[0].ENCLKOS3
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_C[4]              PLL[0].PHASESTEP
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_D[2]              PLL[0].ENCLKOP
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_D[3]              PLL[0].PHASELOADREG
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_D[4]              PLL[0].PHASEDIR
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_LSR[0]            PLL[0].STDBY
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_F[0]               PLL[0].CLKOP
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_F[2]               PLL[0].CLKOS
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_F[4]               PLL[0].CLKOS2
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_F[6]               PLL[0].CLKOS3
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_Q[0]               PLL[0].REFCLK
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_Q[2]               PLL[0].LOCK
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_Q[4]               PLL[0].INTLOCK
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[0]       PLL[0].CLK[0]
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[1]       PLL[0].CLK[1]
			// wire CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0]      PLL[0].CLKFB
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB[0]    PLL[0].CLKINTFB
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0]       PLL[0].CLKOP
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS[0]       PLL[0].CLKOS
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS2[0]      PLL[0].CLKOS2
			// wire CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS3[0]      PLL[0].CLKOS3
		}

		tile_class PLL_SE {
			cell CELL[0];
			cell CELL[1];
			cell IO_WE;
			cell IO_IN;

			switchbox BEL_INT {
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_WE.OUT_IO_INCK[0] | IO_WE.OUT_IO_INCK[2] | IO_IN.OUT_IO_INCK[2];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_CLK[1] | IO_WE.OUT_IO_INCK[0] | IO_WE.OUT_IO_INCK[2] | IO_IN.OUT_IO_INCK[2];
				mux CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0] = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB[0] | CELL[1].IMUX_CLK[0] | IO_WE.ECLK[0] | IO_WE.ECLK[1];
			}

			bel PLL[0] {
				input CLKFB = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKFB[0];
				input RST = CELL[0].IMUX_B[1];
				input CLK[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[0];
				input CLK[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_PLL_CLKI[1];
				input SEL = CELL[0].IMUX_B[2];
				input PHASEDIR = CELL[0].IMUX_D[4];
				input PHASESEL[0] = CELL[0].IMUX_B[4];
				input PHASESEL[1] = CELL[0].IMUX_A[4];
				input PHASESTEP = CELL[0].IMUX_C[4];
				input PHASELOADREG = CELL[0].IMUX_D[3];
				input PLLWAKESYNC = CELL[0].IMUX_C[2];
				input STDBY = CELL[0].IMUX_LSR[0];
				input ENCLKOP = CELL[0].IMUX_D[2];
				input ENCLKOS = CELL[0].IMUX_A[3];
				input ENCLKOS2 = CELL[0].IMUX_B[3];
				input ENCLKOS3 = CELL[0].IMUX_C[3];
				output CLKOP = CELL[0].OUT_F[0], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0];
				output CLKOS = CELL[0].OUT_F[2], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS[0];
				output CLKINTFB = CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKINTFB[0];
				output LOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[2];
				output CLKOS2 = CELL[0].OUT_F[4], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS2[0];
				output CLKOS3 = CELL[0].OUT_F[6], CELL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS3[0];
				output REFCLK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[0];
				output INTLOCK = CELL[0].OUT_Q[4];
			}

		tile_class CLK_W {
			cell CELL_EDGE[0];
			cell CELL_EDGE[1];
			cell CELL_EDGE[2];
			cell CELL_EDGE[3];
			cell CELL_EDGE[4];
			cell CELL_EDGE[5];
			cell CELL_EDGE[6];
			cell CELL_EDGE[7];
			cell CELL_EDGE[8];
			cell CELL_EDGE[9];
			cell CELL_EDGE[10];
			cell CELL_EDGE[11];
			cell CELL_EBR[0];
			cell CELL_EBR[1];
			cell FABRIC_IN_M[0];
			cell FABRIC_IN_M[1];
			cell FABRIC_IN_M[2];
			cell FABRIC_IN_M[3];
			cell PLL[0];
			cell PLL[1];
			cell ROOT;

			bel DLLDEL[0] {
				input A = CELL_EDGE[3].OUT_IO_INCK[2];
				input DDRDEL = CELL_EDGE[5].IMUX_DLLDEL_DDRDEL;
				input DIRECTION = CELL_EDGE[5].IMUX_D[6];
				input LOADN = CELL_EDGE[5].IMUX_B[6];
				input MOVE = CELL_EDGE[5].IMUX_C[6];
				output Z = CELL_EDGE[5].OUT_DLLDEL_Z;
				output CFLAG = CELL_EDGE[5].OUT_Q[7];
			}

			bel DLLDEL[1] {
				input A = CELL_EDGE[3].OUT_IO_INCK[0];
				input DDRDEL = CELL_EDGE[4].IMUX_DLLDEL_DDRDEL;
				input DIRECTION = CELL_EDGE[4].IMUX_D[6];
				input LOADN = CELL_EDGE[4].IMUX_B[6];
				input MOVE = CELL_EDGE[4].IMUX_C[6];
				output Z = CELL_EDGE[4].OUT_DLLDEL_Z;
				output CFLAG = CELL_EDGE[4].OUT_Q[7];
			}

			bel DLLDEL[2] {
				input A = CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_IO_INCK[2];
				input DDRDEL = CELL_EDGE[7].IMUX_DLLDEL_DDRDEL;
				input DIRECTION = CELL_EDGE[7].IMUX_D[6];
				input LOADN = CELL_EDGE[7].IMUX_B[6];
				input MOVE = CELL_EDGE[7].IMUX_C[6];
				output Z = CELL_EDGE[7].OUT_DLLDEL_Z;
				output CFLAG = CELL_EDGE[7].OUT_Q[7];
			}

			bel DLLDEL[3] {
				input A = CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_IO_INCK[0];
				input DDRDEL = CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_DLLDEL_DDRDEL;
				input DIRECTION = CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_D[6];
				input LOADN = CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_B[6];
				input MOVE = CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_C[6];
				output Z = CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_DLLDEL_Z;
				output CFLAG = CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_Q[7];
			}

			bel CLKDIV[0] {
				input CLKI = CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_CLKDIV_CLKI[0];
				input RST = CELL_EBR[0].IMUX_LSR[0];
				input ALIGNWD = CELL_EBR[0].IMUX_A[3];
				output CDIVX = CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_CLKDIV_CDIVX[0], CELL_EBR[0].OUT_F[2];
			}

			bel CLKDIV[1] {
				input CLKI = CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_CLKDIV_CLKI[1];
				input RST = CELL_EBR[0].IMUX_LSR[1];
				input ALIGNWD = CELL_EBR[0].IMUX_B[3];
				output CDIVX = CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_CLKDIV_CDIVX[1], CELL_EBR[0].OUT_F[3];
			}

			switchbox CLK_INT {
				mux CELL_EDGE[4].INCK[0] = CELL_EDGE[3].OUT_IO_INCK[0] | CELL_EDGE[4].OUT_DLLDEL_Z;
				mux CELL_EDGE[4].IMUX_DLLDEL_DDRDEL = CELL_EDGE[0].DDRDEL_S | CELL_EDGE[0].DDRDEL_N;
				mux CELL_EDGE[5].ECLK[0] = CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_ECLKSYNC_ECLKO[0] | CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_ECLKSYNC_ECLKO[2] | ROOT.BRGECLK[0];
				mux CELL_EDGE[5].ECLK[1] = CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_ECLKSYNC_ECLKO[1] | CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_ECLKSYNC_ECLKO[3] | ROOT.BRGECLK[1];
				mux CELL_EDGE[5].INCK[0] = CELL_EDGE[3].OUT_IO_INCK[2] | CELL_EDGE[5].OUT_DLLDEL_Z;
				mux CELL_EDGE[5].IMUX_DLLDEL_DDRDEL = CELL_EDGE[0].DDRDEL_S | CELL_EDGE[0].DDRDEL_N;
				mux CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_DCC_CLKI[0] = CELL_EDGE[2].IMUX_D[7] | CELL_EDGE[5].INCK[0] | FABRIC_IN_M[0].IMUX_CLK[1] | PLL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS2[0] | PLL[1].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0];
				mux CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_DCC_CLKI[1] = CELL_EDGE[0].IMUX_D[7] | CELL_EDGE[4].INCK[0] | CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_CLKDIV_CDIVX[1] | FABRIC_IN_M[1].IMUX_CLK[1] | PLL[1].OUT_PLL_CLKOS[0];
				mux CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_DCC_CLKI[2] = CELL_EDGE[0].OUT_OSC_OSC | CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_CLKDIV_CDIVX[1] | CELL_EDGE[7].INCK[0] | CELL_EDGE[9].IMUX_D[7] | PLL[1].OUT_PLL_CLKOS2[0];
				mux CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_DCC_CLKI[3] = CELL_EDGE[6].INCK[0] | CELL_EDGE[11].IMUX_D[7] | FABRIC_IN_M[3].IMUX_CLK[1] | PLL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOP[0] | PLL[1].OUT_PLL_CLKOS3[0];
				mux CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_DCC_CLKI[4] = CELL_EDGE[6].INCK[0] | CELL_EDGE[6].OUT_CLKDIV_CDIVX[0] | CELL_EDGE[11].IMUX_D[7] | PLL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS3[0] | PLL[1].OUT_PLL_CLKOS2[0];
				mux CELL_EDGE[6].IMUX_DCC_CLKI[5] = CELL_EDGE[0].OUT_OSC_OSC | CELL_EDGE[7].INCK[0] | CELL_EDGE[9].IMUX_D[7] | FABRIC_IN_M[2].IMUX_CLK[1] | PLL[0].OUT_PLL_CLKOS2[0];
